Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any bacteria module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 958. The projective resolution has length at most 565 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_958(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 958. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^565(S, F) is trivial. QED.
